def storage:

  name is array of uint256 at storage 1
  symbol is array of uint256 at storage 2
  decimals is uint256 at storage 3
  adminAddress is address at storage 4
  pendingAdminAddress is address at storage 5
  comptrollerAddress is address at storage 6
  interestRateModelAddress is address at storage 7
  initialExchangeRateMantissa is uint256 at storage 8
  reserveFactorMantissa is uint256 at storage 9
  accrualBlockNumber is uint256 at storage 10
  borrowIndex is uint256 at storage 11
  totalBorrows is uint256 at storage 12
  totalReserves is uint256 at storage 13
  totalSupply is uint256 at storage 14
  balanceOf is mapping of uint256 at storage 15
  allowance is mapping of uint256 at storage 16
  stor17 is mapping of struct at storage 17

  require calldata.size - 4 >= 32
  return balanceOf[address(account)]

def allowance(address owner, address spender): # not payable
  require calldata.size - 4 >= 64
  return allowance[address(owner)][address(spender)]

def admin(): # not payable
